We can derive update for single flip using vectorized arrays:
Dplus length N-1 (r=1..N-1), Dminus length N-1.
For flip p from old to new (0/1). delta = new-old.
For r where p+r<N: Dplus[p+r? wait Dplus[r] includes pair i,i+r. If p is left: contribution old A_p*(1-A_{p+r}); if new. Change = delta*(1-A_{p+r})? Since if delta=1, old0 new1: newA=1, old0 => if right 0, +1; if right1,0. delta*(1-A[right]). If delta=-1: - (1-A[right]). Good.
If p-r>=0: p as right in pair (p-r,p): contribution old A_{p-r}*(1-A_p); new with A_p new. Change = A_{p-r}*(old - new) = -delta * A_{p-r}. So:
Dplus[r] += delta*(1-A[p+r]) for r=1..N-1-p.
Dplus[p-r] -= delta*A[p-r] for r=1..p.
Dminus similarly? Dminus[r] = # i: A_i=0, A_{i+r}=1. This is positive? It is r pairs with left 0 right 1. For flip p as left: contribution (1-A_p)*A_{p+r}; change: if delta=1, left becomes 1 so loses? new (0)*right=0, old (1)*right=right, change = -delta*A_right. If p as right: A_{p-r}*(1-new? wait right p as right in Dminus? Pair left p-r, right p: (1-A_left)*A_p. Change = (1-A_left)*delta. So:
Dminus: left p += -delta*A[p+r]; right p += delta*(1-A[p-r]).
We can also note Dminus[r] for sequence equals Dplus[r] for reverse sequence.

For a flip, update slices with vectorized: if p>0: Dplus[0:p] -= delta * A[:p]? Need align r=p-index: for r in 1..p, A[p-r], array reversed A[p-1::-1]? Use Dplus[0:p] corresponds r=1..p? For left: Dplus[p:N-1-p]? r=1..N-1-p, index p-1? r index = r-1; p+r -> r? Dplus[p:N-p-1] += delta*(1-A[p+1:]). Since index r-1 from p to N-p-2. Good.
Dminus[0:p] += delta*(1-A[p-1::-1]); Dminus[p:N-p-1] += -delta*A[p+1:]. Need check indices: if p=0, Dplus[0:N-1]+=delta*(1-A[1:]), Dminus[0:N-1]-=delta*A[1:]. Good.

Let's derive gradient of smooth max precisely. Let c = correlate(h, 1-h, full). dx factor. Let α. Let w_i = exp(α(c_i - m)) / sum, m=max. Smooth objective F = dx*(m + logsumexp(w?)/α). Gradient = dx * ∑_m w_m ∇c_m. We can compute ∇c_m for all m with weights efficiently using convolutions.

c_m(d) = ∑_{i∈valid} h_i - h_i h_{i+d}.

  if p>0:

Implementation of add_lag_gradient(grad, m, w, h):
  n=h.size; d=m-(n-1); r=abs(d)
  if r==0: grad += w*(1-2*h)
  elif d>0:
     grad[:n-r] += w*(1 - h[r:])
     grad[r:] += -w*h[:n-r]
  else:
     # d=-r
     grad[r:] += w*(1 - h[:n-r])
     grad[:n-r] += -w*h[r:]

def smooth_value_full(h, alpha): c = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); m=c.max(); z=alpha*(c-m); lse = m/alpha? Wait F = (m + logsumexp(z)/alpha)*dx? def solve_coord_fast(c_aff, h, p, h0, unaff, cur_max):
    n=h.size
    # c_aff length n
    # If cur_max - unaff <= 1e-12: return None
    if cur_max <= unaff + 1e-12:
       return None
    Llo=unaff; Lhi=cur_max
    # Precompute arrays for linear constraints
    if p>0:
       hl=h[:p]
       ml=hl>1e-15
       if np.any(ml):
          left_c_over = c_aff[:p][ml] / hl[ml]
          left_inv = 1.0 / hl[ml]
          left_has=True
       else: left_has=False; left_c_over=left_inv=np.array([])
    else: left_has=False
    if p<n-1:
       hr=h[p+1:]
       mr=hr<1.0-1e-15
       if any:
          right_c_over = c_aff[p+1:][mr] / (1-hr[mr])
          right_inv = 1.0/(1-hr[mr])
       else false
    # diag
    c_diag = c_aff[p]
    a0 = 1.0 - 2.0*h0
    # diagonal max possible? Not needed.
    for _ in range(25):
       L=0.5*(Llo+Lhi)
       # feasible interval for δ
       dlo = -h0
       dhi = 1.0 - h0
       if left_has:
          # δ ≥ max(left_c_over - L*left_inv)
          val = np.max(left_c_over - L*left_inv)
          if val > dlo: dlo=val
       if right_has:
          # δ ≤ min(L*right_inv - right_c_over)
          val = np.min(L*right_inv - right_c_over)
          if val < dhi: dhi=val
       # clip dlo/dhi to [ -h0,1-h0 ] (already)
       if dlo > dhi + 1e-13:
          Llo=L; continue
       # diag constraint: f=c_diag + a0 δ - δ^2 ≤ L
       disc = a0*a0 + 4.0*(c_diag - L)
       if disc >= 0:
          sq=np.sqrt(disc)
          r1=(a0 - sq)/2.0
          r2=(a0 + sq)/2.0
          # allowed δ ≤ r1 or δ ≥ r2
          if dlo <= r1 and dhi >= r1? Need check if interval intersects outside.
          # If entire [dlo,dhi] inside (r1,r2) infeasible. Else feasible.
          if dlo > r1 and dhi < r2:
              Llo=L; continue
          # adjust interval to feasible parts
          if dhi > r1 and dlo < r1:
              # keep [dlo,r1] and maybe [r2,dhi]
              # We just need feasibility; no adjust now
              pass
              if dlo > r2 or dhi < r2? We'll later handle choose.
       # feasible
       Lhi=L
    L=Lhi
    # compute final feasible δ interval at L
    dlo=-h0; dhi=1-h0
    if left_has: dlo=max(dlo, np.max(...))
    if right_has: dhi=min(dhi, ...)
    if dlo>dhi: return None
    # apply diag exclusion: If disc>=0 and interval lies inside (r1,r2), no feasible (should not). Else choose point.
    if disc >=0:
       sq... r1,r2
       if dlo > r1 and dhi < r2:
          # no feasible due diag; choose closer endpoint? But should not if L feasible. Maybe due numerical set Lhi.
          if dlo - r1 < r2 - dhi: dlo = r1? Actually choose r1 or r2 outside interval? For feasible L, interval should not be entirely inside. But if due tol, pick r1 or r2.
       # Restrict to [dlo,r1] ∪ [r2,dhi]
       # Choose point in feasible set. If [dlo,r1] nonempty, choose its midpoint maybe; else [r2,dhi]
       if dlo <= r1:
           midpoint = 0.5*(dlo + min(dhi,r1))
           # But if midpoint inside? dlo<=r1 and min(dhi,r1)<=r1, okay.
       elif dhi >= r2:
           midpoint=0.5*(max(dlo,r2)+dhi)
       else:
           # choose closer root outside? pick r1 or r2
           midpoint = r1 if abs(dlo-r1)<=abs(dhi-r2) else r2
       # Ensure within [dlo,dhi]? If midpoint from other side maybe not.
       # Simpler: choose point in [dlo,dhi] that is not in (r1,r2). If dlo<=r1, x=dlo (feasible if dlo<=r1). If dhi>=r2, x=dhi. Else if dlo>=r2, x=dlo. Else x=r2? But x must be within. Let's implement:
       if dlo <= r1:
           δ = dlo
       elif dhi >= r2:
           δ = dhi
       elif dlo >= r2:
           δ = dlo
       else:
           # interval crosses a gap? Actually if dlo>r1 and dhi<r2 impossible feasible; choose root closest to interval
           δ = r1 if abs(dlo-r1) <= abs(dhi-r2) else r2
       if δ < -h0-1e-12 or δ >1-h0+1e-12:
           δ = min(max(δ,-h0),1-h0)
    else:
       δ = 0.5*(dlo+dhi)
    x = h0 + δ
    x = clip(x,0,1)
    return x, L

def project_feasible(y, n):
    y = np.array(y,dtype=float,copy=True)
    if y.min()>=-1e-12 and y.max()<=1+1e-12 and abs(y.sum()-n*0.5)<1e-10:
       y=np.clip(y,0,1); adjust? return y
    low = -np.max(y) - 1.0  # shift such that all 0? If λ low => y+λ ≤0? Need sum≤target. Choose low = -max(y) - 1, high= 1 - min(y)+1.
    # Actually sum(clip(y+λ)) monotone. At low = max(0? If λ=-max(y), max=0, others negative => sum0. At high=1-min(y), min=1 => sum n. Add margins.
    low = -np.max(y) - 1.0
    high = 1.0 - np.min(y) + 1.0
    for 60: mid=(low+high)/2; s=np.clip(y+mid,0,1).sum(); if s < n*0.5: low=mid else high=mid
    return np.clip(y+(low+high)/2,0,1)

def cd_pass(h,n,max_passes? no one pass, order, allow_bound, deadline, tol=1e-13):
  c = correlate...
  cur=c.max()
  improved=False
  for idx,p in enumerate(order):
    if idx&31==0 and time.time()>deadline: break
    h0=h[p]
    if not allow_bound and (h0<1e-12 or h0>1-1e-12): continue
    start=n-1-p; end=start+n
    unaff=0.0
    if start>0: unaff=c[:start].max()
    if end<2*n-1: unaff=max(unaff,c[end:].max())
    if unaff >= cur - 1e-11: continue
    x = solve_coord_fast(c[start:end],h,p,h0,unaff,cur)
    if x is None: continue
    x=np.clip(x,0,1); delta=x-h0
    if abs(delta)<1e-14: continue
    aff=c[start:end].copy()
    if p>0: aff[:p] -= delta*h[:p]
    aff[p] += delta*(1-2*h0) - delta*delta
    if p<n-1: aff[p+1:] += delta*(1-h[p+1:])
    c[start:end]=aff
    h[p]=x
    nm=aff.max(); if unaff>nm: nm=unaff
    if nm < cur:
       cur=nm; improved=True
    else:
       # if not improve but changed? solve should not increase; could due numerical. If nm>cur+1e-9 rollback?
       if nm > cur + 1e-9:
          c[start:end]=aff +? Need rollback. Simpler if solve failed, revert h and c.
  return h,c,cur,improved

def active_order(c, n, top=25, rng=None):

  if len(active)>top: active=active[:top]? Choose random sample: if size>top, rng.choice(size,top)

    if r==0: aff[:]=True
    else:
      aff[:n-r]=True
      aff[r:]=True

  idxs=np.flatnonzero(aff)

Add lag gradient function top-level:
def add_lag_grad(grad, m, w, h, n):
  d=m-(n-1)
  if d==0: grad += w*(1-2*h)
  elif d>0:
    r=d; grad[:n-r] += w*(1-h[r:]); grad[r:] += -w*h[:n-r]
  else:
    r=-d; grad[r:] += w*(1-h[:n-r]); grad[:n-r] += -w*h[r:]
This allocates temporaries. Active lags maybe few.

def solve_coord(cAff, h, p, h0, unaff, curmax):
    if curmax <= unaff + 1e-12: return None
    n=h.size
    Llo, Lhi = unaff, curmax
    left_has = False; right_has=False
    if p:
        hl=h[:p]; ml=hl>1e-15
        if ml.any():
            left_c=cAff[:p][ml]/hl[ml]; left_l=1.0/hl[ml]; left_has=True
    if p<n-1:
        hr=h[p+1:]; mr=hr<1.0-1e-15
        if mr.any():
            right_c=cAff[p+1:][mr]/(1.0-hr[mr]); right_l=1.0/(1.0-hr[mr]); right_has=True
    cd=cAff[p]; ad=1.0-2.0*h0
    for _ in range(26):
       L=0.5*(Llo+Lhi)
       dlo=-h0; dhi=1.0-h0
       if left_has:
          v=np.max(left_c - L*left_l)
          if v>dlo: dlo=v
       if right_has:
          v=np.min(L*right_l - right_c)
          if v<dhi: dhi=v
       if dlo <= dhi + 1e-12:
          disc=ad*ad + 4.0*(cd-L)
          if disc>=0:
             sq=np.sqrt(disc); r1=(ad-sq)*0.5; r2=(ad+sq)*0.5
             if dlo > r1 and dhi < r2:
                Llo=L; continue
          Lhi=L
       else: Llo=L
    L=Lhi
    dlo=-h0; dhi=1.0-h0
    if left_has:
      v=np.max(...); if v>dlo: dlo=v
    if right_has: ...
    if dlo>dhi: return None
    disc=...
    if disc>=0:
      sq... r1,r2
      if dlo <= r1:
         delta=dlo
      elif dhi >= r2:
         delta=dhi
      elif dlo >= r2:
         delta=dlo
      else:
         delta = r1 if abs(dlo-r1) <= abs(dhi-r2) else r2
    else: delta=0.5*(dlo+dhi)
    x=h0+delta
    if x<0: x=0; if x>1: x=1
    # We might want choose midpoint inside feasible, not necessarily endpoint. Endpoint may be more aggressive; okay.
    return x

def smooth_value(h, alpha):
  c = np.correlate(h, 1-h, 'full')
  m = c.max()
  if alpha <= 0: return (m)*(2.0/h.size)
  z = alpha*(c-m)
  # z max 0; sum_exp at least 1
  s = np.exp(z).sum()
  return (m + np.log(s)/alpha)*(2.0/h.size)

def smooth_jac(x, free, base, n, alpha):
  y=base.copy(); y[free]=x
  c=np.correlate(y,1-y,'full'); m=c.max(); w=np.exp(alpha*(c-m)); w/=w.sum()
  # threshold
  grad=np.zeros(n)
  idxs=np.flatnonzero(w > 1e-6)
  h=y
  for m_i in idxs:
     add_lag_grad(grad, m_i, w[m_i], h, n)
  dx=2.0/n
  return dx*grad[free]

Implementation:
def cd_pass(h,c,cur,order,deadline,allow_bound=False):
  n=len(h); target=n/2; improved=False; sum_err=0.0
  M=2*n-1
  count=0
  for p in order:
    if (count&63)==0 and time.time()>deadline: break
    count+=1
    h0=h[p]
    if not allow_bound and (h0<=1e-13 or h0>=1-1e-13): continue
    start=n-1-p; end=start+n
    unaff=0.0
    if start>0:
       u=c[:start].max(); if u>unaff: unaff=u
    if end<M:
       u=c[end:].max(); if u>unaff: unaff=u
    if unaff >= cur - 1e-11: continue
    x=solve_coord(c[start:end], h,p,h0,unaff,cur)
    if x is None: continue
    x=float(np.clip(x,0,1)); delta=x-h0
    if abs(delta)<1e-14: continue
    old=c[start:end].copy()
    aff=old.copy()
    if p>0: aff[:p] -= delta*h[:p]
    aff[p] += delta*(1-2*h0)-delta*delta
    if p<n-1: aff[p+1:] += delta*(1-h[p+1:])
    newmax=aff.max(); if unaff>newmax: newmax=unaff
    if newmax > cur + 1e-9:
       # rollback
       continue
    c[start:end]=aff; h[p]=x; sum_err += delta
    if newmax < cur:
       cur=newmax; improved=True
    if abs(sum_err) > 1.0:
       h=project_feasible(h,n); c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); cur=c.max(); sum_err=0.0
       # cur may be >? If projection worsened, still continue? Could break if cur>oldcur+? But best separate.
  return h,c,cur,improved

Function active_order(c,n,rng, top=20):
  cur=c.max(); active=np.where(c >= cur - 1e-4)[0]
  if active.size>top: active=rng.choice(active, size=top, replace=False)
  aff=np.zeros(n,bool)
  for m in active:
    d=abs(m-(n-1))
    if d==0: aff.fill(True)
    else:
      aff[:n-d]=True; aff[d:]=True
  if not aff.any(): aff[n//2]=True
  idx=np.flatnonzero(aff)
  others=np.setdiff1d(np.arange(n), idx, assume_unique=False) # O(n log n). n small.
  if idx.size>0: rng.shuffle(idx)
  if others.size>0: rng.shuffle(others)
  return np.concatenate((idx,others))
This computes setdiff each pass; okay.

def enforce_sum(h,n):
  target=n/2
  for _ in range(10):
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)<=1e-14: break
    if diff>0:
       idx=np.argmax(1-h)
       inc=min(diff,1-h[idx]); h[idx]+=inc
    else:
       idx=np.argmax(h); dec=min(-diff,h[idx]); h[idx]-=dec
  return h

Detailed helper definitions:

import numpy as np, time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_EQ = [0.0]

def _eq_fun(x): return x.sum() - _EQ[0]
def _eq_jac(x): return np.ones(x.shape)

def count_max(h):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')))
def c5_value(h): return count_max(h)*2.0/len(h)
def full_counts(h): c = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); return c

def enforce_sum(h,n): ...
def project_feasible(y,n):
   y=np.array(y,float,copy=True)
   y[~np.isfinite(y)] = 0.5
   if abs(y.sum()-0.5*n)<1e-12 and y.min()>=-1e-12 and y.max()<=1+1e-12:
       return enforce_sum(np.clip(y,0,1),n)
   lo=-float(np.max(y))-1.0; hi=1.0-float(np.min(y))+1.0
   for _ in range(70):
      mid=0.5*(lo+hi); s=np.clip(y+mid,0,1).sum()
      if s < 0.5*n: lo=mid
      else: hi=mid
   h=np.clip(y+0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
   return enforce_sum(h,n)

enforce_sum:
 def enforce_sum(h,n):
   target=0.5*n
   for _ in range(30):
     diff=target-h.sum()
     if abs(diff)<=1e-15: return h
     if diff>0:
       idx=int(np.argmax(1-h)); step=min(diff,1-h[idx])
       if step<=0: break; h[idx]+=step
     else:
       idx=int(np.argmax(h)); step=min(-diff,h[idx])
       if step<=0: break; h[idx]-=step
   return h

If diff cannot be absorbed due floating, break. Good.

solve_coord:
Need use cAff view; but cAff[p] diagonal. In bisection, left_c etc computed. If left_has false left_c undefined; in final recompute condition. Use local variables default np.array([]).
Code:
def solve_coord(cAff,h,p,h0,unaff,cur):
    if cur <= unaff + 1e-12: return None
    n=len(h)
    Llo=unaff; Lhi=cur
    left_c=left_l=right_c=right_l=None
    if p:
       hl=h[:p]; ml=hl>1e-15
       if ml.any():
          left_c=cAff[:p][ml]/hl[ml]; left_l=1.0/hl[ml]
    if p<n-1:
       hr=h[p+1:]; mr=hr<1.0-1e-15
       if mr.any():
          right_c=cAff[p+1:][mr]/(1.0-hr[mr]); right_l=1.0/(1.0-hr[mr])
    cd=cAff[p]; ad=1.0-2.0*h0
    for _ in range(28):
       L=0.5*(Llo+Lhi)
       dlo=-h0; dhi=1.0-h0
       if left_c is not None:
          v=float(np.max(left_c - L*left_l))
          if v>dlo: dlo=v
       if right_c is not None:
          v=float(np.min(L*right_l - right_c))
          if v<dhi: dhi=v
       if dlo <= dhi + 1e-12:
          disc=ad*ad+4.0*(cd-L)
          if disc>=0:
             r1=(ad-np.sqrt(disc))*0.5; r2=(ad+np.sqrt(disc))*0.5
             if dlo > r1 and dhi < r2:
                Llo=L; continue
          Lhi=L
       else:
          Llo=L
    L=Lhi
    dlo=-h0; dhi=1.0-h0
    if left_c is not None:
       v=float(np.max(left_c - L*left_l)); 
       if v>dlo: dlo=v
    if right_c is not None:
       v=float(np.min(L*right_l - right_c)); 
       if v<dhi: dhi=v
    if dlo > dhi:
       # choose midpoint clipped? return None or h0? return h0 maybe.
       return None
    disc=ad*ad+4.0*(cd-L)
    if disc>=0:
       r1=(ad-np.sqrt(disc))*0.5; r2=(ad+np.sqrt(disc))*0.5
       if dlo <= r1:
          hi=min(dhi,r1); delta=0.5*(dlo+hi)
       elif dhi >= r2:
          lo=max(dlo,r2); delta=0.5*(lo+dhi)
       elif dlo >= r2:
          delta=dlo
       else:
          # infeasible gap; pick closer root
          delta = r1 if abs(dlo-r1) <= abs(dhi-r2) else r2
    else:
       delta=0.5*(dlo+dhi)
    x=h0+delta
    if x<0: x=0.0
    if x>1: x=1.0
    return x

But in final if disc>=0 and interval [dlo,dhi] is partially feasible: if dlo<=r1, hi=min(dhi,r1); if hi<dlo? Could happen if dlo<=r1 but dhi<r1? hi=dhi, ok. If dlo very close r1 and dhi inside (r1,r2)? Then dlo<=r1 and hi=r1, midpoint feasible at dlo/r1. If dlo > r1 and dhi < r2 infeasible, but L shouldn't. The else picks root maybe outside [dlo,dhi]; then x may violate linear constraints because root outside interval? If infeasible due numerical, maybe return None. Could check if delta<dlo-eps or delta>dhi+eps: return None. Add.
After choose delta, if delta<dlo-1e-10 or delta>dhi+1e-10: return None.

cd_pass:
def cd_pass(h,c,cur,order,deadline,allow_bound=False):
    n=len(h); target=n*0.5; M=2*n-1; improved=False; sum_err=0.0; count=0
    for p in order:
       count +=1
       if (count & 127)==0 and time.time()>deadline: break
       h0=h[p]
       if not allow_bound and (h0<=1e-13 or h0>=1.0-1e-13): continue
       start=n-1-p; end=start+n
       unaff=0.0
       if start>0:
          u=float(c[:start].max()); 
          if u>unaff: unaff=u
       if end<M:
          u=float(c[end:].max()); 
          if u>unaff: unaff=u
       if unaff >= cur - 1e-11: continue
       x=solve_coord(c[start:end],h,p,h0,unaff,cur)
       if x is None: continue
       x=float(x); delta=x-h0
       if abs(delta)<1e-14: continue
       aff=c[start:end].copy()
       if p>0: aff[:p] -= delta*h[:p]
       aff[p] += delta*(1.0-2.0*h0)-delta*delta
       if p<n-1: aff[p+1:] += delta*(1.0-h[p+1:])
       nm=float(aff.max())
       if unaff>nm: nm=unaff
       if nm > cur + 1e-8: continue
       c[start:end]=aff; h[p]=x; sum_err += delta
       if nm < cur: cur=nm; improved=True
       if abs(sum_err) > 2.0:
          h=project_feasible(h,n); c=count array; cur=float(c.max()); sum_err=0.0
          if cur >= 1e9: pass
    return h,c,cur,improved

active_order:
def active_order(c,n,rng, top=16):
    cur=float(c.max())
    active=np.where(c >= cur - 1e-4)[0]
    if active.size > top:
       active=rng.choice(active, size=top, replace=False)
    aff=np.zeros(n,bool)
    for m in active:
       d=abs(int(m)-(n-1))
       if d==0:
          aff.fill(True); break
       aff[:n-d]=True
       aff[d:]=True
    if not aff.any(): aff[n//2]=True
    idx=np.flatnonzero(aff)
    others=np.setdiff1d(np.arange(n), idx, assume_unique=False)
    if idx.size: rng.shuffle(idx)
    if others.size: rng.shuffle(others)
    return np.concatenate((idx,others))

np.setdiff1d sorts; okay.

cd_run:
def cd_run(h0,n,deadline,best_c,best_h,rng,allow_bound=False, use_active=True):
    h=h0.copy(); c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); cur=float(c.max())
    if cur < best_c: best_c=cur; best_h=h.copy()
    noimp=0; passno=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
       order=active_order(c,n,rng) if use_active else rng.permutation(n)
       h,c,cur,im=cd_pass(h,c,cur,order,deadline,allow_bound)
       h=project_feasible(h,n)
       c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); cur=float(c.max())
       if cur < best_c:
          best_c=cur; best_h=h.copy()
       if not im:
          noimp +=1
          if noimp >= 3: break
       else: noimp=0
       passno +=1
       # If cur not improving over last 10 passes? track
    return best_h,best_c

Local search code with np arrays. Need ensure `flip_state` update A values. Dp,Dm dtype float. In candidate evaluation, after flip p and q, A updated. If accept, keep A and recompute lists. If reject, revert and lists unchanged.
Pseudo:
def try_binary(hsrc,n,deadline,rng,K):
    A=make_binary(hsrc,n)
    cnp=np.correlate(A,1-A,'full').astype(float)
    Dp=cnp[n:2*n-1]
    Dm=cnp[n-2:0:-1]
    M=max(Dp.max(),Dm.max())
    if M<=K: return A
    best=A.copy(); bestM=M
    ones=np.flatnonzero(A); zeros=np.flatnonzero(1-A)
    noimp=0; temp=1.0; it=0
    while time.time()<deadline and it<50000:
       it+=1
       p=q=None
       if noimp < 80 or M > K + 4:
          act=np.where((Dp==M) | (Dm==M))[0]
          if act.size:
             r=int(act[rng.randint(act.size)])
             if Dp[r] >= M: # because Dp exactly M maybe
                idx=np.nonzero(A[:-r] & (1-A[r:]))[0]
                if idx.size:
                   ii=int(idx[rng.randint(idx.size)]); p=ii; q=ii+r
             elif Dm[r] >= M:
                idx=np.nonzero((1-A[:-r]) & A[r:])[0]
                if idx.size:
                   ii=int(idx[rng.randint(idx.size)]); p=ii+r; q=ii
       if p is None:
          p=int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]); q=int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
          if p==q: # impossible if lengths? maybe if ones=zeros? no
       # evaluate
       flip_state(Dp,Dm,A,p,-1)
       flip_state(Dp,Dm,A,q,+1)
       NM=max(float(Dp.max()),float(Dm.max()))
       if NM < M or (NM == M and rng.random() < 0.15) or (NM > M and rng.random() < math.exp(-((NM-M))/temp)):
           M=NM
           # update lists by recompute (A already swapped)
           ones=np.flatnonzero(A); zeros=np.flatnonzero(1-A)
           if M < bestM:
              bestM=M; best=A.copy()
              if M<=K: return best
           noimp=0
           temp=max(0.03, temp*0.9995)
       else:
           flip_state(Dp,Dm,A,q,-1)
           flip_state(Dp,Dm,A,p,+1)
           noimp +=1
           if noimp>50: temp=max(0.01,temp*0.98)
    return best

flip_state with A updated and Dp/Dm float:
def flip_state(Dp,Dm,A,p,delta):
    n=A.size
    A[p] = A[p] + delta
    if p>0:
       left=A[p-1::-1]
       Dp[:p] -= delta * left
       Dm[:p] += delta * (1.0 - left)
    if p+1<n:
       right=A[p+1:]
       Dp[p:n-1-p] += delta * (1.0 - right)
       Dm[p:n-1-p] -= delta * right
Test with n=3,A=[1,0,1], p=1 (1->0 delta=-1). Dp initially r1: pair (0,1):1,0 count1; r2 none? n-1-p=1. Dm r1: 0? left0 right1 at (1,2) A1=0,A2=1 =>1. Flip p=1 to0:
 left A[0]=1: Dp[:1] -= -1*1 => +1? But Dp r1 was 1 (1,0) old? Wait old A[1]=0? I set A=[1,0,1], p=1 is 0 not 1. Bad. Let's test p=0 1->0 delta=-1: right=A[1:]=[0,1]; Dp[0:2] += -1*(1-right) = -[1,0] -> Dp r1 was 1 ->0? Old pairs: (0,1) 1->0 count1; after p0=0, count0. good. Dm[0:2] -= -1*right = +[0,1] -> Dm r1 old 0? old (0,2)? r2 (0,2) 1->1 count0; after 0,1? p0=0, A2=1 -> Dm r2 +1. correct.
Good.

SLSQP functions with add_lag_grad:
def add_lag_grad(grad,m,w,h,n):
    d=m-(n-1)
    if d==0:
       grad += w*(1.0-2.0*h)
    elif d>0:
       r=d
       grad[:n-r] += w*(1.0-h[r:])
       grad[r:] += -w*h[:n-r]
    else:
       r=-d
       grad[r:] += w*(1.0-h[:n-r])
       grad[:n-r] += -w*h[r:]
Need grad length n. In SLSQP jac, if many active lags, grad becomes dx*sum weights. Good.

smooth_subset_obj/jac:
def smooth_obj(x, free, base, n, alpha):
    y=base.copy(); y[free]=x
    c=np.correlate(y,1-y,'full')
    m=float(c.max())
    if alpha <= 1.0:
       return (m + 0.0)? Actually if alpha small, average; use logsumexp.
    z=alpha*(c-m)
    s=float(np.exp(z).sum())
    return (m + np.log(s)/alpha)*(2.0/n)
For alpha maybe not too large (200k). z for nonmax can be -200k -> exp underflow 0, sum=1, fine.

def smooth_jac(x, free, base,n,alpha):
    y=base.copy(); y[free]=x
    c=np.correlate(y,1-y,'full'); m=float(c.max())
    w=np.exp(alpha*(c-m)); w/=w.sum()
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    thresh=1e-5
    idx=np.flatnonzero(w>thresh)
    for mi in idx:
       add_lag_grad(grad, int(mi), float(w[mi]), y, n)
    return (2.0/n)*grad[free]

Could be heavy if alpha small and many idx. Use alphas large: 5000, 50000, 500000.

select_free:
def select_free(h,c,n,maxfree=250):
    cur=float(c.max())
    active=np.where(c >= cur - 1e-3)[0]
    if active.size > 20: active=active[:20]
    score=np.zeros(n)
    for mi in active:
       g=np.zeros(n)
       add_lag_grad(g,int(mi),1.0,h,n)
       score += np.abs(g)
    # If score all zero, use coordinates near 0.5? 
    if score.max() <= 1e-14:
       score = np.exp(-((h-0.5)/0.1)**2) # no lambda, okay array
    # Add small random? Not.
    mask=score > 1e-12
    cnt=int(mask.sum())
    if cnt == 0:
       idx=np.arange(n)
    elif cnt > maxfree:
       idx_c=np.flatnonzero(mask)
       part=np.argpartition(score[idx_c], -maxfree)[-maxfree:]
       idx=idx_c[part]
    else:
       idx=np.flatnonzero(mask)
    if idx.size < 10:
       # pad with most interior coordinates
       interior=np.argsort(np.abs(h-0.5))[:max(10-idx.size,10)]
       idx=np.union1d(idx, interior)
    return idx.astype(int)

np.argsort sorts ascending closeness. Good.

run_slsqp:
def run_slsqp(starts, deadline, rng):
    best_h=None; best_c=1e9
    for h0 in starts:
       if time.time() > deadline - 45: break
       h=np.array(h0,dtype=float); n=h.size
       if n != len? okay
       h=project_feasible(h,n)
       c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); cur=float(c.max())
       if cur*2/n < best_c: best_c=cur*2/n; best_h=h.copy()
       # Try up to alphas until deadline
       for alpha in [5000,50000,500000]:
          if time.time() > deadline - 20: break
          free=select_free(h,c,n,250)
          if free.size < 5: continue
          _EQ[0]=float(h[free].sum())
          bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*free.size
          sub=x0=h[free].copy()
          try:
             res=minimize(smooth_obj, sub, args=(free,h,n,alpha), method='SLSQP', jac=smooth_jac,
                         bounds=bounds, constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':_eq_fun,'jac':_eq_jac}],
                         options={'maxiter':60,'ftol':1e-12})
             yh=h.copy(); yh[free]=res.x
             yh=project_feasible(yh,n); cn=np.correlate(yh,1-yh,'full'); cv=float(cn.max())
             if cv < cur*1.0001:
                h=yh; c=cn; cur=float(c.max())
                if cv*2/n < best_c: best_c=cv*2/n; best_h=h.copy()
          except Exception:
             pass
       # final CD? not here
    return best_h,best_c

Interpolation:
def interp_to(h,n):
    if len(h)==n: return h.copy()
    xs=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,len(h),endpoint=False)
    xt=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n,endpoint=False)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(xt,xs,h),n)

make_binary:
def make_binary(hsrc,n):
    y=interp_to(hsrc,n) if len(hsrc)!=n else hsrc.copy()
    target=n//2
    B=np.zeros(n)
    if target>0:
       idx=np.argpartition(-y, target)[:target]
       B[idx]=1.0
    return B
argpartition with target=n//2. If target==n? not.

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
 t0=time.time(); dl=t0+max(1,budget_s)-6
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 # parent
 try:
    h_best=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
 except Exception:
    h_best=np.full(600,0.5)
 n_best=len(h_best); h_best=project_feasible(h_best,n_best)
 best_c=compute_c5(h_best)
 # If parent sum not? done.
 # 1. CD parent
 cd_deadline=min(dl, t0 + max(60, int(0.48*budget_s)))  # ~528 for1100
 h1,c1=cd_run(h_best,n_best,cd_deadline,rng,allow_bound=False)
 if c1<best_c: h_best,best_c=h1,c1
 # 2. Binary
 bin_deadline=min(dl, time.time()+120)
 binary_starts=[]
 for ntry in [n_best, 800]:
    if time.time() > bin_deadline - 5: break
    if ntry % 2 !=0: continue
    K=int(np.floor(0.3808*ntry/2.0 + 1e-9))
    hsrc = h_best if ntry==n_best else interp_to(h_best,ntry)
    B=try_binary(hsrc,ntry, min(bin_deadline, time.time()+ (90 if ntry==n_best else 30)), rng,K)
    if B is not None:
       cB=compute_c5(B)
       if cB < best_c:
          return B, float(cB), ntry
       # If cB not better, keep as start if not too worse? maybe if cB < best_c+0.002
       if cB < best_c + 0.002:
          binary_starts.append(B)
 # 3. Scaled/binary CD starts
 start_h_list=[]
 for s in [0.7,1.2,1.6,2.4]:
    start_h_list.append(scale_h(h_best,s))
 start_h_list.extend(binary_starts)
 # allocate time based on remaining to SLSQP maybe 250
 slsqp_reserve = 180
 final_reserve = 60
 multi_deadline = min(dl - slsqp_reserve - final_reserve, time.time() + max(60, int(0.25*budget_s)))
 for hs in start_h_list:
    time_left = multi_deadline - time.time()
    if time_left < 30: break
    per = min(60.0, max(15.0, time_left / max(1,len(start_h_list))))
    d=min(multi_deadline, time.time()+per)
    h2,c2=cd_run(hs,len(hs),d,rng,allow_bound=True)
    if c2<best_c: h_best,best_c=h2,c2
 # 4. SLSQP
 slsqp_dl=min(dl - final_reserve, time.time()+slsqp_reserve)
 sl_starts=[h_best]
 # maybe include one scaled? h_best enough.
 hs,cs=run_slsqp(sl_starts, slsqp_dl, rng)
 if cs<best_c: h_best,best_c=hs,cs
 # 5. final CD
 h3,c3=cd_run(h_best,len(h_best),min(dl,time.time()+final_reserve),rng,allow_bound=False)
 if c3<best_c: h_best,best_c=h3,c3
 h_best=project_feasible(h_best,len(h_best))
 best_c=compute_c5(h_best)
 return h_best,best_c,len(h_best)

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_EQ=[0.0]
def _ef(x): return x.sum()-_EQ[0]
def _ej(x): return np.ones(x.shape)

def fmax(h): return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')))
def cv(h): return fmax(h)*2.0/h.size
def enforce_sum(h,n):
    t=0.5*n
    for _ in range(30):
       d=t-h.sum()
       if abs(d)<=1e-15: return h
       if d>0:
          i=int(np.argmax(1-h)); s=min(d,1-h[i])
       else:
          i=int(np.argmax(h)); s=min(-d,h[i])
       if s<=0: break
       h[i]+=s
    return h
def project_feasible(y,n):
    y=np.array(y,float,copy=True)
    bad=~np.isfinite(y); y[bad]=0.5
    if y.min()>=-1e-12 and y.max()<=1+1e-12 and abs(y.sum()-0.5*n)<1e-10:
       return enforce_sum(np.clip(y,0,1),n)
    lo=-float(np.max(y))-1.0; hi=1.0-float(np.min(y))+1.0
    for _ in range(70):
       m=0.5*(lo+hi); s=float(np.clip(y+m,0,1).sum())
       if s < 0.5*n: lo=m
       else: hi=m
    return enforce_sum(np.clip(y+0.5*(lo+hi),0,1),n)
```

solve:
```
def solve_coord(cA,h,p,h0,unaff,cur):
    if cur <= unaff + 1e-12: return None
    n=h.size
    lc=ll=rc=rl=None
    if p:
       hl=h[:p]; ml=hl>1e-15
       if ml.any(): lc=cA[:p][ml]/hl[ml]; ll=1.0/hl[ml]
    if p<n-1:
       hr=h[p+1:]; mr=hr<1.0-1e-15
       if mr.any(): rc=cA[p+1:][mr]/(1.0-hr[mr]); rl=1.0/(1.0-hr[mr])
    cd=cA[p]; ad=1.0-2.0*h0
    Llo,Lhi=unaff,cur
    for _ in range(28):
       L=0.5*(Llo+Lhi)
       dlo=-h0; dhi=1.0-h0
       if lc is not None:
          v=float(np.max(lc-L*ll)); 
          if v>dlo: dlo=v
       if rc is not None:
          v=float(np.min(L*rl-rc)); 
          if v<dhi: dhi=v
       if dlo <= dhi + 1e-12:
          disc=ad*ad+4.0*(cd-L)
          if disc>=0:
             ss=np.sqrt(disc); r1=0.5*(ad-ss); r2=0.5*(ad+ss)
             if dlo>r1 and dhi<r2: 
                Llo=L; continue
          Lhi=L
       else: Llo=L
    L=Lhi
    dlo=-h0; dhi=1.0-h0
    if lc is not None:
       v=float(np.max(lc-L*ll)); 
       if v>dlo: dlo=v
    if rc is not None:
       v=float(np.min(L*rl-rc)); 
       if v<dhi: dhi=v
    if dlo>dhi: return None
    disc=ad*ad+4.0*(cd-L)
    if disc>=0:
       ss=np.sqrt(disc); r1=0.5*(ad-ss); r2=0.5*(ad+ss)
       if dlo<=r1:
          delta=0.5*(dlo+min(dhi,r1))
       elif dhi>=r2:
          delta=0.5*(max(dlo,r2)+dhi)
       elif dlo>=r2:
          delta=dlo
       else:
          delta=r1 if abs(dlo-r1)<=abs(dhi-r2) else r2
    else:
       delta=0.5*(dlo+dhi)
    if delta<dlo-1e-10 or delta>dhi+1e-10: return None
    x=h0+delta
    if x<0: x=0.0
    elif x>1: x=1.0
    return x
```
Good.

cd_pass:
```
def cd_pass(h,c,cur,order,deadline,allow=False):
    n=h.size; M=2*n-1; improved=False; serr=0.0; cnt=0
    for p in order:
       cnt+=1
       if (cnt&127)==0 and time.time()>deadline: break
       h0=h[p]
       if not allow and (h0<=1e-13 or h0>=1-1e-13): continue
       st=n-1-p; en=st+n
       unaff=0.0
       if st>0:
          u=float(c[:st].max())
          if u>unaff: unaff=u
       if en<M:
          u=float(c[en:].max())
          if u>unaff: unaff=u
       if unaff >= cur - 1e-10: continue
       x=solve_coord(c[st:en],h,p,h0,unaff,cur)
       if x is None: continue
       x=float(x); d=x-h0
       if abs(d)<1e-14: continue
       aff=c[st:en].copy()
       if p>0: aff[:p]-=d*h[:p]
       aff[p]+=d*(1.0-2*h0)-d*d
       if p<n-1: aff[p+1:]+=d*(1.0-h[p+1:])
       nm=float(aff.max())
       if unaff>nm: nm=unaff
       if nm>cur+1e-8: continue
       c[st:en]=aff; h[p]=x; serr+=d
       if nm<cur:
          cur=nm; improved=True
       if abs(serr)>2.0:
          h=project_feasible(h,n); c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full'); cur=float(c.max()); serr=0.0
    return h,c,cur,improved
```
If mid projection worsens cur, improved remains maybe. okay.

active_order:
```
def active_order(c,n,rng,top=16):
    cur=float(c.max())
    act=np.where(c>=cur-1e-4)[0]
    if act.size>top: act=rng.choice(act,size=top,replace=False)
    aff=np.zeros(n,bool)
    for m in act:
       d=abs(int(m)-(n-1))
       if d==0:
          aff.fill(True); break
       aff[:n-d]=True; aff[d:]=True
    if not aff.any(): aff[n//2]=True
    idx=np.flatnonzero(aff)
    oth=np.setdiff1d(np.arange(n),idx,assume_unique=False)
    if idx.size: rng.shuffle(idx)
    if oth.size: rng.shuffle(oth)
    return np.concatenate((idx,oth))
```

cd_run:
```
def cd_run(h0,n,deadline,rng,allow=False):
    h=np.array(h0,float,copy=True)
    if h.size!=n: h=project_feasible(interp_to(h,n),n) ??? define interp before? Or assume n passed consistent.
    h=project_feasible(h,len(h)); n=len(h)
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full'); cur=float(c.max())
    bh=h.copy(); bc=cur*2.0/n
    noimp=0
    while time.time()<deadline:
       order=active_order(c,n,rng)
       h,c,cur,im=cd_pass(h,c,cur,order,deadline,allow)
       h=project_feasible(h,n); c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full'); cur=float(c.max()); val=cur*2.0/n
       if val<bc:
          bc=val; bh=h.copy()
       if not im:
          noimp+=1
          if noimp>=3: break
       else: noimp=0
    return bh,bc
```
Need define interp_to before cd_run? h0 size n; okay. If h0 size not n, project? We can just use h0 size. In run pass correct n.

interp_to define before cd_run maybe after project.

Binary:
```
def interp_to(h,n):
    if h.size==n: return h.copy()
    xs=np.linspace(0,2,h.size,endpoint=False); xt=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(xt,xs,h),n)
```
Define before make_binary and cd_run? cd_run doesn't use.

make_binary:
```
def make_binary(h,n):
    y=interp_to(h,n)
    t=n//2
    b=np.zeros(n)
    if t>0:
       b[np.argpartition(-y,t)[:t]]=1.0
    return b
```

flip_state:
```
def flip_state(Dp,Dm,A,p,dl):
    n=A.size; A[p]+=dl
    if p>0:
       left=A[p-1::-1]
       Dp[:p]-=dl*left; Dm[:p]+=dl*(1.0-left)
    if p+1<n:
       right=A[p+1:]
       Dp[p:n-1-p]+=dl*(1.0-right); Dm[p:n-1-p]-=dl*right
```

try_binary:
```
def try_binary(hsrc,n,deadline,rng,K):
    A=make_binary(hsrc,n)
    cf=np.correlate(A,1.0-A,'full').astype(float)
    Dp=cf[n:2*n-1].copy(); Dm=cf[n-2:0:-1].copy()
    M=max(float(Dp.max()),float(Dm.max()))
    if M<=K: return A
    best=A.copy(); bestM=M
    ones=np.flatnonzero(A); zeros=np.flatnonzero(1.0-A)
    noimp=0; temp=1.0; it=0
    while time.time()<deadline and it<30000:
       it+=1
       p=None; q=None
       if noimp<80 or M>K+4:
          act=np.where((Dp==M)|(Dm==M))[0]
          if act.size:
             ri=int(act[rng.randint(act.size)]); dist=ri+1
             if Dp[ri]>=M:
                idx=np.nonzero(A[:-dist] & (1.0-A[dist:]))[0]
                if idx.size:
                   ii=int(idx[rng.randint(idx.size)]); p=ii; q=ii+dist
             elif Dm[ri]>=M:
                idx=np.nonzero((1.0-A[:-dist]) & A[dist:])[0]
                if idx.size:
                   ii=int(idx[rng.randint(idx.size)]); p=ii+dist; q=ii
       if p is None:
          p=int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]); q=int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
       flip_state(Dp,Dm,A,p,-1.0); flip_state(Dp,Dm,A,q,1.0)
       NM=max(float(Dp.max()),float(Dm.max()))
       if NM<M or (NM==M and rng.random()<0.12) or (NM>M and rng.random()<math.exp(-((NM-M)/max(temp,1e-3)))):
          M=NM; ones=np.flatnonzero(A); zeros=np.flatnonzero(1.0-A)
          if M<bestM: bestM=M; best=A.copy(); if M<=K: return best
          noimp=0; temp=max(0.03,temp*0.9995)
       else:
          flip_state(Dp,Dm,A,q,-1.0); flip_state(Dp,Dm,A,p,1.0)
          noimp+=1
          if noimp>50: temp=max(0.01,temp*0.98)
    return best
```
Syntax `if M<=K: return best` cannot same line with semicolon and if? In Python can `if M < bestM: bestM=M; best=A.copy(); if M<=K: return best` invalid because if after semicolon in same line? You can do simple statements separated by semicolon but a compound statement `if` cannot follow semicolon on same line? Actually `if cond: stmt; if cond2: return` is one line? It is two simple statements? The second `if` is compound, not allowed after semicolon? Better put on next line or use `if M < bestM: bestM=M; best=A.copy(); 
 if M<=K: return best` (newline). We'll write multiple lines.

Potential bug: When reject, flip back q then p. After swap state p=0,q=1. Revert q 1->0 (dl=-1), then p 0->1 (dl=+1). Good.
When accept, A state p=0,q=1, D counted. ones/zeros recomputed. Good.

```
def scale_h(h,s): return project_feasible(0.5+(h-0.5)*s, h.size)
```

SLSQP functions as above. add_lag_grad:
```
def add_lag_grad(g,m,w,h,n):
    d=m-(n-1)
    if d==0: g += w*(1.0-2.0*h)
    elif d>0:
       r=d; g[:n-r] += w*(1.0-h[r:]); g[r:] += -w*h[:n-r]
    else:
       r=-d; g[r:] += w*(1.0-h[:n-r]); g[:n-r] += -w*h[r:]
```

smooth_obj:
```
def smooth_obj(x,free,base,n,alpha):
    y=base.copy(); y[free]=x
    c=np.correlate(y,1.0-y,'full'); m=float(c.max())
    z=alpha*(c-m); return (m+np.log(float(np.exp(z).sum()))/alpha)*(2.0/n)
```
If alpha huge, z for nonmax underflow, sum≥1. If alpha <=0 not used.
smooth_jac similar.
Select_free as above. Need `maxfree` int.
```
def select_free(h,c,n,maxfree=220):
    cur=float(c.max()); act=np.where(c>=cur-1e-3)[0]
    if act.size>20: act=act[:20]
    score=np.zeros(n)
    for m in act:
       g=np.zeros(n); add_lag_grad(g,int(m),1.0,h,n); score += np.abs(g)
    if score.max()<=1e-14:
       score=np.exp(-((h-0.5)/0.1)**2)
    mask=score>1e-12; cnt=int(mask.sum())
    if cnt==0:
       idx=np.arange(n)
    elif cnt>maxfree:
       ic=np.flatnonzero(mask); part=np.argpartition(score[ic], -maxfree)[-maxfree:]; idx=ic[part]
    else: idx=np.flatnonzero(mask)
    if idx.size<10:
       idx=np.union1d(idx, np.argsort(np.abs(h-0.5))[:max(10-idx.size,10)])
    return idx.astype(int)
```
np.argpartition with maxfree; if maxfree > cnt? only when cnt>maxfree. Good.

run_slsqp:
```
def run_slsqp(starts,deadline,rng):
    bh=None; bc=1e9
    for h0 in starts:
       if time.time()>deadline-45: break
       h=project_feasible(np.array(h0,float,copy=True), len(h0)); n=h.size
       c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full'); cur=float(c.max())
       if cur*2.0/n<bc: bc=cur*2.0/n; bh=h.copy()
       for alpha in (5000,50000,200000):
          if time.time()>deadline-25: break
          free=select_free(h,c,n,220)
          if free.size<8: continue
          _EQ[0]=float(h[free].sum())
          try:
             res=minimize(smooth_obj, h[free].copy(), args=(free,h,n,alpha), method='SLSQP', jac=smooth_jac,
                         bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*free.size,
                         constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':_ef,'jac':_ej}],
                         options={'maxiter':60,'ftol':1e-12})
             yh=h.copy(); yh[free]=res.x; yh=project_feasible(yh,n)
             cyh=np.correlate(yh,1.0-yh,'full'); cy=float(cyh.max())
             if cy < cur*1.0005:
                h=yh; c=cyh; cur=cy
                if cy*2.0/n<bc: bc=cy*2.0/n; bh=h.copy()
          except Exception:
             pass
    return bh,bc
```

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); dl=t0+max(1,int(budget_s))-8
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try: h_best=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception: h_best=np.full(600,0.5)
    n_best=h_best.size
    h_best=project_feasible(h_best,n_best)
    best_c=cv(h_best)
    # Maybe evaluate initial before project? project may slightly increase? Use after.
    cd_dl=min(dl, t0+max(60,int(0.38*budget_s)))
    h1,c1=cd_run(h_best,n_best,cd_dl,rng,False)
    if c1<best_c: h_best,best_c=h1,c1
    binary_starts=[]
    if budget_s>550:
       bin_dl=min(dl, time.time()+120)
       for ntry in (n_best,800):
          if ntry%2 or time.time()>bin_dl-3: continue
          src=h_best if ntry==n_best else interp_to(h_best,ntry)
          d=bin_dl if ntry==n_best else min(bin_dl,time.time()+40)
          K=int(np.floor(0.3808*ntry/2.0+1e-9))
          B=try_binary(src,ntry,d,rng,K)
          if B is not None:
             cB=cv(B)
             if cB<best_c:
                return B, float(cB), ntry
             if cB < best_c + 0.002:
                binary_starts.append(B)
    # multi starts
    starts=[]
    for s in (0.7,1.15,1.6,2.4):
       starts.append(scale_h(h_best,s))
    starts.extend(binary_starts)
    multi_dl=min(dl-260, time.time()+max(60,int(0.22*budget_s)))
    for hs in starts:
       if time.time()>multi_dl-25 or len(hs)>2000: break
       per=min(55.0, max(12.0, (multi_dl-time.time())/(len(starts)+1)))
       h2,c2=cd_run(hs, len(hs), min(multi_dl, time.time()+per), rng, True)
       if c2<best_c: h_best,best_c=h2,c2
    # SLSQP
    sl_dl=min(dl-45, time.time()+200)
    if time.time()<sl_dl-30:
       h4,c4=run_slsqp([h_best], sl_dl, rng)
       if c4 and c4<best_c: h_best,best_c=h4,c4
    # final cd
    final_dl=min(dl, time.time()+45)
    h5,c5=cd_run(h_best,len(h_best),final_dl,rng,False)
    if c5<best_c: h_best,best_c=h5,c5
    h_best=project_feasible(h_best,len(h_best))
    best_c=cv(h_best)
    return h_best, float(best_c), h_best.size
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_EQ = [0.0]
_SL = [None, None, None, None]

def _ef(x):
    return x.sum() - _EQ[0]

def _ej(x):
    return np.ones(x.shape)

def fmax(h):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')))

def cv(h):
    return fmax(h) * 2.0 / h.size

def enforce_sum(h, n):
    t = 0.5 * n
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    for _ in range(30):
        d = t - h.sum()
        if abs(d) <= 1e-15:
            return h
        if d > 0:
            i = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            s = min(d, 1.0 - h[i])
        else:
            i = int(np.argmax(h))
            s = min(-d, h[i])
        if s <= 0.0:
            break
        h[i] += s
    return h

def project_feasible(y, n):
    y = np.array(y, dtype=float, copy=True)
    bad = ~np.isfinite(y)
    y[bad] = 0.5
    if y.min() >= -1e-12 and y.max() <= 1.0 + 1e-12 and abs(y.sum() - 0.5 * n) < 1e-10:
        return enforce_sum(np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0), n)
    lo = -float(np.max(y)) - 1.0
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(y)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
        m = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.clip(y + m, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if s < 0.5 * n:
            lo = m
        else:
            hi = m
    return enforce_sum(np.clip(y + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0), n)

def solve_coord(cA, h, p, h0, unaff, cur):
    if cur <= unaff + 1e-12:
        return None
    n = h.size
    lc = ll = None
    rc = rl = None
    if p:
        hl = h[:p]
        ml = hl > 1e-15
        if ml.any():
            lc = cA[:p][ml] / hl[ml]
            ll = 1.0 / hl[ml]
    if p < n - 1:
        hr = h[p + 1:]
        mr = hr < 1.0 - 1e-15
        if mr.any():
            rc = cA[p + 1:][mr] / (1.0 - hr[mr])
            rl = 1.0 / (1.0 - hr[mr])
    cd = cA[p]
    ad = 1.0 - 2.0 * h0
    Llo, Lhi = unaff, cur
    for _ in range(28):
        L = 0.5 * (Llo + Lhi)
        dlo = -h0
        dhi = 1.0 - h0
        if lc is not None:
            v = float(np.max(lc - L * ll))
            if v > dlo:
                dlo = v
        if rc is not None:
            v = float(np.min(L * rl - rc))
            if v < dhi:
                dhi = v
        if dlo <= dhi + 1e-12:
            disc = ad * ad + 4.0 * (cd - L)
            if disc >= 0.0:
                ss = np.sqrt(disc)
                r1 = 0.5 * (ad - ss)
                r2 = 0.5 * (ad + ss)
                if dlo > r1 and dhi < r2:
                    Llo = L
                    continue
            Lhi = L
        else:
            Llo = L
    L = Lhi
    dlo = -h0
    dhi = 1.0 - h0
    if lc is not None:
        v = float(np.max(lc - L * ll))
        if v > dlo:
            dlo = v
    if rc is not None:
        v = float(np.min(L * rl - rc))
        if v < dhi:
            dhi = v
    if dlo > dhi:
        return None
    disc = ad * ad + 4.0 * (cd - L)
    if disc >= 0.0:
        ss = np.sqrt(disc)
        r1 = 0.5 * (ad - ss)
        r2 = 0.5 * (ad + ss)
        if dlo <= r1:
            delta = 0.5 * (dlo + min(dhi, r1))
        elif dhi >= r2:
            delta = 0.5 * (max(dlo, r2) + dhi)
        elif dlo >= r2:
            delta = dlo
        else:
            delta = r1 if abs(dlo - r1) <= abs(dhi - r2) else r2
    else:
        delta = 0.5 * (dlo + dhi)
    if delta < dlo - 1e-10 or delta > dhi + 1e-10:
        return None
    x = h0 + delta
    if x < 0.0:
        x = 0.0
    elif x > 1.0:
        x = 1.0
    return float(x)

def cd_pass(h, c, cur, order, deadline, allow=False):
    n = h.size
    M = 2 * n - 1
    improved = False
    serr = 0.0
    cnt = 0
    for p in order:
        cnt += 1
        if (cnt & 127) == 0 and time.time() > deadline:
            break
        h0 = h[p]
        if not allow and (h0 <= 1e-13 or h0 >= 1.0 - 1e-13):
            continue
        st = n - 1 - p
        en = st + n
        unaff = 0.0
        if st > 0:
            u = float(c[:st].max())
            if u > unaff:
                unaff = u
        if en < M:
            u = float(c[en:].max())
            if u > unaff:
                unaff = u
        if unaff >= cur - 1e-10:
            continue
        x = solve_coord(c[st:en], h, p, h0, unaff, cur)
        if x is None:
            continue
        d = x - h0
        if abs(d) < 1e-14:
            continue
        aff = c[st:en].copy()
        if p > 0:
            aff[:p] -= d * h[:p]
        aff[p] += d * (1.0 - 2.0 * h0) - d * d
        if p < n - 1:
            aff[p + 1:] += d * (1.0 - h[p + 1:])
        nm = float(aff.max())
        if unaff > nm:
            nm = unaff
        if nm > cur + 1e-8:
            continue
        c[st:en] = aff
        h[p] = x
        serr += d
        if nm < cur:
            cur = nm
            improved = True
        if abs(serr) > 2.0:
            h = project_feasible(h, n)
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')
            cur = float(c.max())
            serr = 0.0
    return h, c, cur, improved

def active_order(c, n, rng, top=16):
    cur = float(c.max())
    act = np.where(c >= cur - 1e-4)[0]
    if act.size > top:
        act = rng.choice(act, size=top, replace=False)
    aff = np.zeros(n, bool)
    for m in act:
        d = abs(int(m) - (n - 1))
        if d == 0:
            aff.fill(True)
            break
        aff[:n - d] = True
        aff[d:] = True
    if not aff.any():
        aff[n // 2] = True
    idx = np.flatnonzero(aff)
    oth = np.setdiff1d(np.arange(n), idx, assume_unique=False)
    if idx.size:
        rng.shuffle(idx)
    if oth.size:
        rng.shuffle(oth)
    return np.concatenate((idx, oth))

def interp_to(h, n):
    if h.size == n:
        return h.copy()
    xs = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, h.size, endpoint=False)
    xt = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(xt, xs, h), n)

def cd_run(h0, n, deadline, rng, allow=False):
    h = project_feasible(np.array(h0, dtype=float, copy=True), len(h0))
    n = h.size
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')
    cur = float(c.max())
    bh = h.copy()
    bc = cur * 2.0 / n
    noimp = 0
    while time.time() < deadline:
        order = active_order(c, n, rng)
        h, c, cur, im = cd_pass(h, c, cur, order, deadline, allow)
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')
        cur = float(c.max())
        val = cur * 2.0 / n
        if val < bc:
            bc = val
            bh = h.copy()
        if not im:
            noimp += 1
            if noimp >= 3:
                break
        else:
            noimp = 0
    return bh, bc

def make_binary(h, n):
    y = interp_to(h, n)
    t = n // 2
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if t > 0:
        b[np.argpartition(-y, t)[:t]] = 1.0
    return b

def flip_state(Dp, Dm, A, p, dl):
    n = A.size
    A[p] += dl
    if p > 0:
        left = A[p - 1::-1]
        Dp[:p] -= dl * left
        Dm[:p] += dl * (1.0 - left)
    if p + 1 < n:
        right = A[p + 1:]
        Dp[p:n - 1 - p] += dl * (1.0 - right)
        Dm[p:n - 1 - p] -= dl * right

def try_binary(hsrc, n, deadline, rng, K):
    A = make_binary(hsrc, n)
    cf = np.correlate(A, 1.0 - A, 'full').astype(float)
    Dp = cf[n:2 * n - 1].copy()
    Dm = cf[n - 2::-1].copy()
    M = max(float(Dp.max()), float(Dm.max()))
    if M <= K:
        return A
    best = A.copy()
    bestM = M
    ones = np.flatnonzero(A > 0.5)
    zeros = np.flatnonzero(A < 0.5)
    noimp = 0
    temp = 1.0
    it = 0
    while time.time() < deadline and it < 30000:
        it += 1
        p = q = None
        if noimp < 80 or M > K + 4:
            act = np.where((Dp == M) | (Dm == M))[0]
            if act.size:
                ri = int(act[rng.randint(act.size)])
                dist = ri + 1
                if Dp[ri] >= M:
                    idx = np.nonzero((A[:-dist] > 0.5) & (A[dist:] < 0.5))[0]
                    if idx.size:
                        ii = int(idx[rng.randint(idx.size)])
                        p = ii
                        q = ii + dist
                elif Dm[ri] >= M:
                    idx = np.nonzero((A[:-dist] < 0.5) & (A[dist:] > 0.5))[0]
                    if idx.size:
                        ii = int(idx[rng.randint(idx.size)])
                        p = ii + dist
                        q = ii
        if p is None:
            p = int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
            q = int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
        flip_state(Dp, Dm, A, p, -1.0)
        flip_state(Dp, Dm, A, q, 1.0)
        NM = max(float(Dp.max()), float(Dm.max()))
        if NM < M or (NM == M and rng.random() < 0.12) or (NM > M and rng.random() < math.exp(-((NM - M) / max(temp, 1e-3)))):
            M = NM
            ones = np.flatnonzero(A > 0.5)
            zeros = np.flatnonzero(A < 0.5)
            if M < bestM:
                bestM = M
                best = A.copy()
                if M <= K:
                    return best
            noimp = 0
            temp = max(0.03, temp * 0.9995)
        else:
            flip_state(Dp, Dm, A, q, -1.0)
            flip_state(Dp, Dm, A, p, 1.0)
            noimp += 1
            if noimp > 50:
                temp = max(0.01, temp * 0.98)
    return best

def scale_h(h, s):
    return project_feasible(0.5 + (h - 0.5) * s, h.size)

def add_lag_grad(g, m, w, h, n):
    d = m - (n - 1)
    if d == 0:
        g += w * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
    elif d > 0:
        r = d
        g[:n - r] += w * (1.0 - h[r:])
        g[r:] += -w * h[:n - r]
    else:
        r = -d
        g[r:] += w * (1.0 - h[:n - r])
        g[:n - r] += -w * h[r:]

def _sl_obj(x):
    free, base, n, alpha = _SL
    y = base.copy()
    y[free] = x
    c = np.correlate(y, 1.0 - y, 'full')
    m = float(c.max())
    z = alpha * (c - m)
    return (m + np.log(float(np.exp(z).sum())) / alpha) * (2.0 / n)

def _sl_jac(x):
    free, base, n, alpha = _SL
    y = base.copy()
    y[free] = x
    c = np.correlate(y, 1.0 - y, 'full')
    m = float(c.max())
    w = np.exp(alpha * (c - m))
    w /= w.sum()
    g = np.zeros(n)
    idx = np.flatnonzero(w > 1e-5)
    for mi in idx:
        add_lag_grad(g, int(mi), float(w[mi]), y, n)
    return (2.0 / n) * g[free]

def select_free(h, c, n, maxfree=220):
    cur = float(c.max())
    act = np.where(c >= cur - 1e-3)[0]
    if act.size > 20:
        act = act[:20]
    score = np.zeros(n)
    for mi in act:
        g = np.zeros(n)
        add_lag_grad(g, int(mi), 1.0, h, n)
        score += np.abs(g)
    if score.max() <= 1e-14:
        score = np.exp(-((h - 0.5) / 0.1) ** 2)
    mask = score > 1e-12
    cnt = int(mask.sum())
    if cnt == 0:
        idx = np.arange(n)
    elif cnt > maxfree:
        ic = np.flatnonzero(mask)
        part = np.argpartition(score[ic], -maxfree)[-maxfree:]
        idx = ic[part]
    else:
        idx = np.flatnonzero(mask)
    if idx.size < 10:
        idx = np.union1d(idx, np.argsort(np.abs(h - 0.5))[:max(10 - idx.size, 10)])
    return idx.astype(int)

def run_slsqp(starts, deadline, rng):
    bh = None
    bc = 1e9
    for h0 in starts:
        if time.time() > deadline - 45:
            break
        h = project_feasible(np.array(h0, dtype=float, copy=True), len(h0))
        n = h.size
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')
        cur = float(c.max())
        if cur * 2.0 / n < bc:
            bc = cur * 2.0 / n
            bh = h.copy()
        for alpha in (5000, 50000, 200000):
            if time.time() > deadline - 25:
                break
            free = select_free(h, c, n, 220)
            if free.size < 8:
                continue
            _SL[0] = free
            _SL[1] = h
            _SL[2] = n
            _SL[3] = alpha
            _EQ[0] = float(h[free].sum())
            try:
                res = minimize(_sl_obj, h[free].copy(), jac=_sl_jac, method='SLSQP',
                               bounds=[(0.0, 1.0)] * free.size,
                               constraints=[{'type': 'eq', 'fun': _ef, 'jac': _ej}],
                               options={'maxiter': 60, 'ftol': 1e-12})
                yh = h.copy()
                yh[free] = res.x
                yh = project_feasible(yh, n)
                cyh = np.correlate(yh, 1.0 - yh, 'full')
                cy = float(cyh.max())
                if cy < cur * 1.0005:
                    h = yh
                    c = cyh
                    cur = cy
                    if cy * 2.0 / n < bc:
                        bc = cy * 2.0 / n
                        bh = h.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass
    return bh, bc

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    dl = t0 + max(1, int(budget_s)) - 8
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h_best = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        h_best = np.full(600, 0.5)
    n_best = h_best.size
    h_best = project_feasible(h_best, n_best)
    best_c = cv(h_best)

    cd_dl = min(dl, t0 + max(60, int(0.38 * budget_s)))
    h1, c1 = cd_run(h_best, n_best, cd_dl, rng, False)
    if c1 < best_c:
        h_best, best_c = h1, c1

    binary_starts = []
    if budget_s > 550:
        bin_dl = min(dl, time.time() + 120)
        for ntry in (n_best, 800):
            if ntry % 2 or time.time() > bin_dl - 3:
                continue
            src = h_best if ntry == n_best else interp_to(h_best, ntry)
            d = bin_dl if ntry == n_best else min(bin_dl, time.time() + 40)
            K = int(np.floor(0.3808 * ntry / 2.0 + 1e-9))
            B = try_binary(src, ntry, d, rng, K)
            if B is not None:
                cB = cv(B)
                if cB < best_c:
                    return B, float(cB), ntry
                if cB < best_c + 0.002:
                    binary_starts.append(B)

    starts = [scale_h(h_best, s) for s in (0.7, 1.15, 1.6, 2.4)]
    starts.extend(binary_starts)

    multi_dl = min(dl - 260, time.time() + max(60, int(0.22 * budget_s)))
    for hs in starts:
        if time.time() > multi_dl - 25 or hs.size > 2000:
            break
        per = min(55.0, max(12.0, (multi_dl - time.time()) / (len(starts) + 1)))
        h2, c2 = cd_run(hs, hs.size, min(multi_dl, time.time() + per), rng, True)
        if c2 < best_c:
            h_best, best_c = h2, c2

    sl_dl = min(dl - 45, time.time() + 200)
    if time.time() < sl_dl - 30:
        h4, c4 = run_slsqp([h_best], sl_dl, rng)
        if c4 < best_c:
            h_best, best_c = h4, c4

    final_dl = min(dl, time.time() + 45)
    h5, c5 = cd_run(h_best, h_best.size, final_dl, rng, False)
    if c5 < best_c:
        h_best, best_c = h5, c5

    h_best = project_feasible(h_best, h_best.size)
    best_c = cv(h_best)
    return h_best, float(best_c), h_best.size
```
